Each rule earned through real experience (post-mortem, drift detection, user correction).\n4\t> Rules here have higher status than convention but lower than constitution.\n5\t\n6\t## Rule Format\n7\t\n8\tUse this template:\n9\t\n10\t```markdown\n11\t### YYYY-MM-DD: [Short rule name]\n12\t**Context**: What situation triggered learning this\n13\t**Rule**: The actionable rule\n14\t**Anti-example**: What was done wrong\n15\t**Correct example**: What should be done instead\n16\t**Severity**: critical | high | medium | low\n17\t**Auto-detect**: yes/no — can a drift signal catch this?\n18\t```\n19\t\n20\t---\n21\t\n22\t## Initial Rules (from charter design)\n23\t\n24\t### 2026-04-23: LLM Never Outputs Numbers\n25\t**Context**: Stock domain risk — LLM-generated numbers in finance lead to real money loss\n26\t**Rule**: LLM never returns numerical values as natural-language output. All numbers come from deterministic Python functions invoked via tool use. LLM only interprets results.\n27\t**Anti-example**:\n28\t```\n29\tLLM output: \"Based on my analysis, the company's ROE is approximately 18%, P/E is around 12, and intrinsic value is roughly 95,000 VND per share.\"\n30\t```\n31\t**Correct example**:\n32\t```\n33\tLLM tool calls:\n34\t  compute_roe(net_income=2_345B, equity=13_120B) → 17.87%\n35\t  compute_pe(price=85_000, eps=7_054) → 12.05\n36\t  compute_intrinsic_value_dcf(...) → 94_523 VND\n37\tLLM output: \"ROE = 17.87% (FY2024), P/E = 12.05 (TTM), DCF intrinsic value = 94,523 VND. Versus current price 85,000 VND, suggests ~11% margin of safety.\"\n38\t```\n39\t**Severity**: critical\n40\t**Auto-detect**: yes — regex for \"approximately\", \"around\", \"roughly\" + numbers in LLM output\n41\t\n42\t### 2026-04-23: Bear Case Required for Every Thesis\n43\t**Context**: Confirmation bias is the #1 retail investor mistake\n44\t**Rule**: Every thesis output must include substantive bear case (≥3 distinct points, each grounded in evidence). System refuses to render thesis without it.\n45\t**Anti-example**: Thesis with bull case + risks section that says \"market volatility, regulatory changes\" (boilerplate)\n46\t**Correct example**: Bear case with specific points like \"Q3 inventory turnover dropped 23% YoY suggesting demand weakness\", \"Major competitor X launched directly competitive product Y in March 2026\", \"Net working capital deterioration of 15% indicates cash flow strain\"\n47\t**Severity**: high\n48\t**Auto-detect**: yes — bear case length + specificity check\n49\t\n50\t### 2026-04-23: Confidence Requires Calibration Data\n51\t**Context**: LLM \"confidence\" is qualitative; user reads numerical values\n52\t**Rule**: Any \"X% confidence\" or \"high/medium/low confidence\" claim must trace to historical hit rate from calibration database. Include `n_samples`, `lookback_period`, `hit_rate` metadata.\n53\t**Anti-example**: \"70% confidence this is a buy\"\n54\t**Correct example**: \"Confidence: MEDIUM (signal pattern: cyclical_bottom_with_dividend; historical n=23 cases since 2018; hit rate 65% over 12 months; lookback=78 months)\"\n55\t**Severity**: high\n56\t**Auto-detect**: partial — flag confidence claims without metadata\n57\t\n58\t### 2026-04-23: Source Provider Disagreement Surfaced\n59\t**Context**: vnstock vs FiinPro vs TCBS sometimes disagree on same data point\n60\t**Rule**: When sources disagree by >1%, output shows divergence explicitly. Never silently pick one.\n61\t**Anti-example**: \"Revenue Q3 2024: 35,000 tỷ\" (silent average or arbitrary pick)\n62\t**Correct example**: \"Revenue Q3 2024: 35,000 tỷ ± 200 tỷ (vnstock: 35,000 / FiinPro: 35,200 / TCBS: 34,800; reconciled with HIGH confidence)\"\n63\t**Severity**: medium\n64\t**Auto-detect**: no (requires source metadata in output template)\n65\t\n66\t---\n67\t\n68\t## Add New Rules Here\n69\t\n70\t### 2026-04-29: Q&A Bundle Skeleton MUST Carry `defer_cycle` Field\n71\t**Context**: While drafting `qa-escalation/references/sample-bundle.md` in S2, I initially omitted `defer_cycle` from the bundle frontmatter — caught on review against Decision 002 § Track 2 R7 mitigation.\n72\t**Rule**: Every Q&A bundle file in `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/` MUST include `defer_cycle: 0` at creation, and increment on every reopening / supersession. SessionStart hook (Track 5) keys can-kicking detection off this field.\n73\t**Anti-example**: Bundle with frontmatter `id / topic / opened_at / expected_answer_by / priority / status` — passes superficial validation but R7 mitigation is invisible.\n74\t**Correct example**: Bundle frontmatter includes `defer_cycle: 0` from creation; every Decision in `related_decisions:` also has `defer_cycles` field syncronized.\n75\t**Severity**: medium\n76\t**Auto-detect**: yes — Track 5 `qa-pending-stale-mover.sh` hook should reject bundles missing the field.\n77\t\n78\t### 2026-04-29: User Communication Idiom — \"ok rồi. continue\" / \"ok continue\" = Trivial Acknowledgment\n79\t**Context**: User in `human-workspace/user_prompt/20260429_03.txt` and via chat replies repeatedly used the idiom \"ok rồi. continue\" or \"ok continue\" as the standard \"I accept the proposed defaults; proceed\" signal in Q&A round acceptance flows.\n80\t**Rule**: Treat both phrases as TRIVIAL acknowledgment in lite-detect (`user-prompt-intake/references/lite-detect-patterns.md`). Do NOT dispatch intent-classifier on these. They specifically mean \"accept all defaults of the most recent agent-proposed bundle / decision\".\n81\t**Anti-example**: Spending 5K context to classify \"ok continue\" via subagent — wasteful and misses the user's stable communication idiom.\n82\t**Correct example**: Lite-detect short-circuits these to `primary_intent: TRIVIAL` + `recommended_action: HANDLE_TRIVIAL`. Continue per `current-execution.md`.\n83\t**Severity**: low (efficiency, not correctness)\n84\t**Auto-detect**: yes — already covered in lite-detect whitelist as \"special idiom\" entry.\n85\t\n86\t### 2026-04-29: Hooks Invoked by Harness Shell Don't Traverse Claude Permission Model\n87\t**Context**: While designing Q&A escalation in S2, I encountered a tension: `Write(human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/**)` is deny-listed for the agent (only humans move files there), but Track 5 hooks may need to write `status: processed` flag inside answered files.\n88\t**Rule**: Hooks invoked by Claude Code harness (configured in `.claude/settings.json` `hooks` section) execute as shell scripts OUTSIDE the agent's tool-permission model. The deny-list applies to the agent's Write/Edit tool calls, not to hook scripts. Therefore hook scripts CAN write to deny-listed paths, but should still respect the spirit (don't pretend to be human-authored content).\n89\t**Anti-example**: Refusing to write a Track 5 hook that updates `status: processed` because \"the agent can't write to answered/\".\n90\t**Correct example**: Hook script writes the `status: processed` flag; agent's tool calls remain blocked. If unsure, prefer sidecar files (`<bundle>.processed`) over in-place edits to keep audit trail crisp.\n91\t**Severity**: medium\n92\t**Auto-detect**: no (design-time consideration)\n93\t\n94\t### 2026-04-29 (post-audit): AP-S2-3 — False Self-Attestation on Measurable Properties\n95\t**Context**: In S2 session log \"verifier-lite pass\" I claimed: \"SKILL.md bodies are 130-160 LOC each…within target\". Adversarial drift audit (background `general-purpose` subagent) measured actual LOC and found `qa-escalation` = 242 (+61% over) and `grill-maximization` = 187 (+25% over). My self-attestation was wrong by ~25-61%, while feeling confident.\n96\t**Rule**: For any property that can be measured deterministically (LOC, byte count, presence-of-field, file-exists), VERIFY with the actual command (`wc -l`, `Glob`, `Grep`) before writing the verifier-lite claim. Do NOT estimate from memory of \"what I just wrote feels about right.\"\n97\t**Anti-example**: \"manual count: SKILL.md bodies are 130-160 LOC each…within target\" written without running `wc -l`.\n98\t**Correct example**: Run `wc -l <file>` for each verified item; quote the actual number. Q-S5 (\"small trusted circle = git-fork single-tenant\") is identity-tier (locks Phase 0-5 architecture, threshold 0.99). The other 4 (Q-S1..Q-S4) were SCOPE-tier (threshold 0.90). User's \"ok continue\" reply absorbed all 5 via the safe-defaults shortcut. This is exactly the orch CF-DOGFOOD-2 pattern UP-02 §1.1 warned about.\n104\t**Rule**: When composing a Q&A bundle, if ANY question is CHARTER-tier (threshold 0.99), split it into a dedicated CHARTER-only bundle. The CHARTER bundle has: explicit-pick options only (no safe agent-defaults), no `D: open answer`-only fields, ALERT notification regardless of priority, and `approval_chain` requires verbatim user phrase quoted in the answered file (not just `ACCEPTED via \"ok continue\"`).\n105\t**Anti-example**: 5 mixed-tier questions in one bundle accepted via \"ok continue\".\n106\t**Correct example**: 4 SCOPE questions in one bundle (defaults OK); 1 CHARTER question in a separate dedicated bundle with explicit confirmation.\n107\t**Severity**: high (charter scope drift is the most damaging drift type)\n108\t**Auto-detect**: yes — drift-detector grep for bundles where ≥1 `level: CHARTER` field appears alongside other levels in the same `related_decisions:` array.\n109\t\n110\t### 2026-04-29 (UP-06): NO Silent File-Defaults — Every Needed Answer via AskUserQuestion Multi-Batch\n111\t**Context**: User in chat after UP-05 turn explicitly corrected: \"tôi sẽ không vào file q&a để trả lời, ví dụ như tôi đang remote-control từ mobile thì sao. cần phải làm cho q&a thực sự hiệu quả chứ không phải làm cho có\". Agent had been writing bundles with N>4 questions, firing 4 via `AskUserQuestion`, then claiming \"rest will default after 24h if user doesn't edit file\". This is exactly the orch CF-DOGFOOD-2 silent-absorption pattern in disguise — for mobile-remote users especially, file-edit is not a real channel.\n112\t**Rule**: Every question agent NEEDS answered MUST be surfaced via `AskUserQuestion`. For >4 questions: multi-batch chain (within-turn `Ask(4) → Ask(4) → ...` or across-turn). Bundle file = pure audit trail; NEVER an input surface with default-after-N-hours fallback. If a question is \"would be nice but not blocking now\" → DO NOT bundle as deferred-default; instead queue in `agent-workspace/memory/observations/queued-grill-<topic>.md` with `fire_when: <track-or-condition>` trigger. Mobile-remote user never edits file → defaults silently absorb 12 decisions agent never explicitly verified.\n114\t**Correct example**: 16-question bundle → fire 4 via `Ask` → user answers → fire next 4 via `Ask` → ... 4 batches total OR drop questions that aren't truly blocking now (queue them with `fire_when:`). Bundle file logs all 16 with answers from each Ask round; NO \"default\" semantic.\n115\t**Severity**: critical (mobile-remote primary use case; silent absorption is the pattern user explicitly corrected for the third time in this project — UP-02 §1.1, UP-04 G1, UP-06 here).\n116\t**Auto-detect**: yes — drift-detector greps Q&A bundle frontmatter for `pending_in_file:` array OR \"default applies\" string; flag any bundle that has unanswered questions without matching `AskUserQuestion` call OR `queued-grill-*.md` follow-up.\n117\t\n118\t### 2026-04-29 (UP-05): AskUserQuestion 4-Question Limit is Intentional; Multi-Batch is the Workaround\n119\t**Context**: User asked if 4-question limit on `AskUserQuestion` could be bypassed via setting/script/repo. `claude-code-guide` subagent research (2026-04-29) confirmed: limit is intentional design (`maxItems: 4` JSONSchema constraint); NO config flag / env var / hidden setting overrides it.\n120\t**Rule**: For bundles >4 questions, use multi-batch pattern: fire 4 most-critical via `AskUserQuestion` in same turn → record answers → fire next 4 via second `AskUserQuestion` call (or queue file-based for async). No -e (need rc capture per test).\n17\tset -uo pipefail\n18\t\n19\tSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\n20\tPER_TEST_TIMEOUT=\"${FIRING_TEST_TIMEOUT:-30}\"\n21\t\n22\t# Detect timeout(1) availability (POSIX-non-portable; GNU coreutils ubiquitous on Linux/git-bash).\n23\tif command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n24\t  USE_TIMEOUT=1\n25\telse\n26\t  USE_TIMEOUT=0\n27\t  echo \"WARN: timeout(1) not available; running without per-test timeout (slow tests may stall suite)\"\n28\tfi\n29\t\n30\tPASS=0\n31\tFAIL=0\n32\tFAILED_TESTS=\"\"\n33\tTOTAL=0\n34\tSTART_TS=\"$(date +%s 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n35\t\n36\tfor test in \"$SCRIPT_DIR\"/*-fire-test.sh; do\n37\t  [ ! -f \"$test\" ] && continue\n38\t  bn=\"$(basename \"$test\")\"\n39\t  TOTAL=$((TOTAL + 1))\n40\t\n41\t  if [ \"$USE_TIMEOUT\" = 1 ]; then\n42\t    timeout \"$PER_TEST_TIMEOUT\" bash \"$test\" >/dev/null 2>&1\n43\t  else\n44\t    bash \"$test\" >/dev/null 2>&1\n45\t  fi\n46\t  rc=$?\n47\t\n48\t  if [ \"$rc\" = 0 ]; then\n49\t    PASS=$((PASS + 1))\n50\t  else\n51\t    FAIL=$((FAIL + 1))\n52\t    if [ \"$rc\" = 124 ] && [ \"$USE_TIMEOUT\" = 1 ]; then\n53\t      FAILED_TESTS=\"${FAILED_TESTS}  ✗ ${bn} (TIMEOUT >${PER_TEST_TIMEOUT}s)\"$'\\n'\n54\t    else\n55\t      FAILED_TESTS=\"${FAILED_TESTS}  ✗ ${bn} (rc=${rc})\"$'\\n'\n56\t    fi\n57\t  fi\n58\tdone\n59\t\n60\tEND_TS=\"$(date +%s 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n61\tif [ \"$START_TS\" != \"unknown\" ] && [ \"$END_TS\" != \"unknown\" ]; then\n62\t  ELAPSED=$((END_TS - START_TS))\n63\telse\n64\t  ELAPSED=\"?\"\n65\tfi\n66\t\n67\techo \"=== firing-test suite: ${PASS}/${TOTAL} PASS (elapsed ${ELAPSED}s) ===\"\n68\tif [ \"$FAIL\" -gt 0 ]; then\n69\t  echo \"\"\n70\t  echo \"Failed tests:\"\n71\t  printf '%s' \"$FAILED_TESTS\"\n72\t  exit 1\n73\tfi\n74\texit 0\n75\t"}]},"uuid":"e754e288-48b9-4a14-9c49-5e891b7b149b","timestamp":"2026-05-06T17:55:19.158Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\run-all.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# run-all.sh — orchestrator for firing-test suite under scripts/hooks/firing-tests/.\n#\n# Purpose: empirically validate \"N/N PASS\" claims from session close notes per\n# Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence). Each *-fire-test.sh exits 0 on\n# pass, non-zero on fail. This script iterates all of them with a per-test timeout,\n# tallies results, and returns 0 only if ALL pass.\n#\n# Authored S121 (2026-05-06) to close S120 close-note \"69/69 PASS\" unverifiable claim\n# (no orchestrator existed). Each fire-test self-isolates via TEMPDIR + traps; this\n# runner does not stage fixtures itself.\n#\n# Wires: NOT auto-wired to Stop hook (would slow every turn). Run manually post-hook-edit\n# or via `bash scripts/hooks/firing-tests/run-all.sh` to verify suite state.\n#\n# Phase 0 portability: bash + POSIX only (L-S11-1). No -e (need rc capture per test).\nset -uo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nPER_TEST_TIMEOUT=\"${FIRING_TEST_TIMEOUT:-30}\"\n\n# Detect timeout(1) availability (POSIX-non-portable; GNU coreutils ubiquitous on Linux/git-bash).\nif command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  USE_TIMEOUT=1\nelse\n  USE_TIMEOUT=0\n  echo \"WARN: timeout(1) not available; running without per-test timeout (slow tests may stall suite)\"\nfi\n\nPASS=0\nFAIL=0\nFAILED_TESTS=\"\"\nTOTAL=0\nSTART_TS=\"$(date +%s 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n\nfor test in \"$SCRIPT_DIR\"/*-fire-test.sh; do\n  [ ! -f \"$test\" ] && continue\n  bn=\"$(basename \"$test\")\"\n  TOTAL=$((TOTAL + 1))\n\n  if [ \"$USE_TIMEOUT\" = 1 ]; then\n    timeout \"$PER_TEST_TIMEOUT\" bash \"$test\" >/dev/null 2>&1\n  else\n    bash \"$test\" >/dev/null 2>&1\n  fi\n  rc=$?\n\n  if [ \"$rc\" = 0 ]; then\n    PASS=$((PASS + 1))\n  else\n    FAIL=$((FAIL + 1))\n    if [ \"$rc\" = 124 ] && [ \"$USE_TIMEOUT\" = 1 ]; then\n      FAILED_TESTS=\"${FAILED_TESTS}  ✗ ${bn} (TIMEOUT >${PER_TEST_TIMEOUT}s)\"$'\\n'\n    else\n      FAILED_TESTS=\"${FAILED_TESTS}  ✗ ${bn} (rc=${rc})\"$'\\n'\n    fi\n  fi\ndone\n\nEND_TS=\"$(date +%s 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\nif [ \"$START_TS\" != \"unknown\" ] && [ \"$END_TS\" != \"unknown\" ]; then\n  ELAPSED=$((END_TS - START_TS))\nelse\n  ELAPSED=\"?\"\nfi\n\necho \"=== firing-test suite: ${PASS}/${TOTAL} PASS (elapsed ${ELAPSED}s) ===\"\nif [ \"$FAIL\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"\"\n  echo \"Failed tests:\"\n  printf '%s' \"$FAILED_TESTS\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nexit 0\n","numLines":75,"startLine":1,"totalLines":75}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f3d171f3-bbdb-4049-b6e5-d0ef63d9bf3f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"7459a326-32d4-4497-ad57-75434a9c43d8","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"06ba6f1b-d065-45a1-8f4e-9ed5ee99ad30","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_014mqPyTtwEN7qbMN9PRSG9H","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CR1t7pLZuH2K1LErqpSZ4i","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\sync-grilling-call.sh","old_string":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# sync-grilling-call.sh — wrapper for sync-tracker-update.sh with 5-arg contract.\n#\n# Per M-S98-1 prevention rule 3 (S98) + M-S101-1 same-day recurrence (S101 deterministic\n# guard shipped in sync-tracker-update.sh:53-62). This wrapper adds defense-in-depth by\n# hiding the $3 OVERRIDE_DELTA optional positional arg, eliminating the arg-position\n# confusion that caused both M-S98-1 and M-S101-1 (long reason text or decision_id\n# landing in $3 OVERRIDE_DELTA slot, awk replay coercing non-numeric to 0).\n#\n# Contract (5 positional args, all required):\n#   $1 category         e.g. SCOPE | LANGUAGE | DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS | DESIGN_THINKING | DECISION_ROUTING\n#   $2 event_type       e.g. charter_match | drift_signal | q_and_a_resolution | decision_correctness\n#   $3 decision_id      e.g. sync-grilling-S102 (no spaces; identifies the event)\n#   $4 source_evidence  file path to source (e.g. agent-workspace/memory/sync-state.md)\n#   $5 reason           free-form text describing why this event was recorded\n#\n# Behavior: always passes \"\" (empty) as $3 OVERRIDE_DELTA to sync-tracker-update.sh,\n# triggering the weights.yaml `weight_<event_type>` lookup. Override mode is unsupported\n# in this wrapper (use sync-tracker-update.sh directly for non-default deltas).\n#\n# Usage:\n#   sync-grilling-call.sh SCOPE charter_match sync-grilling-S102 \\\n#     agent-workspace/memory/sync-state.md \\\n#     \"S102 sync-grilling refresher: routine auto-tier; ...\"\n#\n# Phase 0 portability per L-S11-1: bash + POSIX only.\n# Per L-S65-2 (harness priority #1): this is a routine convenience layer, NOT a\n# replacement for the deterministic guard already in sync-tracker-update.sh.\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nCAT=\"${1:-}\"\nEVT=\"${2:-}\"\nDEC_ID=\"${3:-}\"\nSRC_EV=\"${4:-}\"\nREASON=\"${5:-}\"\n\nif [ -z \"$CAT\" ] || [ -z \"$EVT\" ] || [ -z \"$DEC_ID\" ] || [ -z \"$SRC_EV\" ] || [ -z \"$REASON\" ]; then\n  cat >&2 <<USAGE\n[sync-grilling-call] usage: $0 <category> <event_type> <decision_id> <source_evidence> <reason>\n\nAll 5 args required. Example:\n  $0 SCOPE charter_match sync-grilling-S102 \\\\\n     agent-workspace/memory/sync-state.md \\\\\n     \"S102 sync-grilling refresher: routine auto-tier; ...\"\n\nCategories: LANGUAGE | DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS | DESIGN_THINKING | SCOPE | DECISION_ROUTING\nEvent types: decision_correctness | charter_match | q_and_a_resolution\n             drift_signal | decision_correction | decision_revocation | thesis_revocation\n\nPer M-S98-1 + M-S101-1 prevention: this wrapper hides the \\$3 OVERRIDE_DELTA arg to\nprevent the arg-position confusion that caused both mistakes. For non-default deltas,\ncall sync-tracker-update.sh directly.\nUSAGE\n  exit 2\nfi\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")\" && pwd)\"\nUPDATE_HOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/sync-tracker-update.sh\"\nif [ ! -f \"$UPDATE_HOOK\" ]; then\n  echo \"[sync-grilling-call] sync-tracker-update.sh not found at $UPDATE_HOOK\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\n\n# Always pass \"\" as $3 OVERRIDE_DELTA → underlying script uses weight_<event_type> from weights.yaml.\nexec bash \"$UPDATE_HOOK\" \"$CAT\" \"$EVT\" \"\" \"$DEC_ID\" \"$SRC_EV\" \"$REASON\"","new_string":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# sync-grilling-call.sh — wrapper for sync-tracker-update.sh with 5-arg contract.\n#\n# Per M-S98-1 prevention rule 3 (S98) + M-S101-1 same-day recurrence (S101 deterministic\n# guard shipped in sync-tracker-update.sh:53-62). This wrapper adds defense-in-depth by\n# hiding the $3 OVERRIDE_DELTA optional positional arg, eliminating the arg-position\n# confusion that caused both M-S98-1 and M-S101-1 (long reason text or decision_id\n# landing in $3 OVERRIDE_DELTA slot, awk replay coercing non-numeric to 0).\n#\n# S129 enhancement (L-S69-1 2nd-instance promote-to-hook): on successful invocation,\n# also updates `last_check:` + `last_check_session:` fields in\n# agent-workspace/memory/sync-state.md frontmatter. Eliminates recurring drift between\n# events.tsv (canonical) + sync-state.md narrative (supplementary). Pattern 1st-instance\n# observed S80 (M-S67-3-style under-recording, manual backfill at S83); 2nd-instance\n# S86-S128 (15 wrapper events without narrative update). Best-effort — wrapper does not fail if sync-state.md\n# missing/malformed (events.tsv canonical; narrative supplementary).\n#\n# Contract (5 positional args, all required):\n#   $1 category         e.g. SCOPE | LANGUAGE | DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS | DESIGN_THINKING | DECISION_ROUTING\n#   $2 event_type       e.g. charter_match | drift_signal | q_and_a_resolution | decision_correctness\n#   $3 decision_id      e.g. sync-grilling-S102 (no spaces; identifies the event)\n#   $4 source_evidence  file path to source (e.g. agent-workspace/memory/sync-state.md)\n#   $5 reason           free-form text describing why this event was recorded\n#\n# Behavior:\n#   1.
